# History Form 5, Form Five History: authored answers for review

Every answer fixed by a person, not computed. Part 1: the tables, conventions and rules that computed answers come from.
Part 2: every authored question template, with all its data (keys, statements, pairs, choices and feedback) and sources.
Regenerate with `npm run review:f5-history`. Item IDs (TR-…) match `docs/teacher-review.md`.

## Part 1

Rules the computed answers use are listed in the subject’s library tests; every authored answer is in Part 2.

Compare that with the description asked for. · constitutional advance concerns an expansion of internal governing responsibility before sovereignty. Compare that with the description asked for. · source perspective concerns a speaker’s institutional position shaping what a source can establish. Compare that with the description asked for. | Ghana’s independence preceded Nigeria’s. |
| Which term or event fits this description: a speaker’s institutional position shaping what a source can establish? | source perspective | nationalist mobilisation · constitutional advance · independence | nationalist mobilisation concerns African organisation and pressure for self-government. Compare that with the description asked for. · constitutional advance concerns an expansion of internal governing responsibility before sovereignty. Compare that with the description asked for. · independence concerns the transfer to sovereign status in the Ghana case. - Feedback `w0`: “{X.e[0]}”
- Feedback `w1`: “{X.e[1]}”
- Feedback `w2`: “{X.e[2]}”

  _↳ feedback if chosen: independence concerns the transfer to sovereign status in the Ghana case. Compare that with the description asked for._

- ◻️ nationalist mobilisation

- ◻️ independence  
  _↳ feedback if chosen: independence concerns the transfer to sovereign status in the Ghana case. Compare that with the description asked for._
- ✅ constitutional advance
- ◻️ source perspective  
  _↳ feedback if chosen: source perspective concerns a speaker’s institutional position shaping what a source can establish. Compare that with the description asked for._

Full working after a second miss:

> Reserved authority distinguished autonomy from full sovereignty.  
> So the answer is “constitutional advance”.  

> Which term or event fits this description: the transfer to sovereign status in the Ghana case?

- ◻️ constitutional advance  
  _↳ feedback if chosen: constitutional advance concerns an expansion of internal governing responsibility before sovereignty. Compare that with the description asked for._
- ◻️ nationalist mobilisation  
  _↳ feedback if chosen: nationalist mobilisation concerns African organisation and pressure for self-government. Compare that with the description asked for._
- ◻️ source perspective  
  _↳ feedback if chosen: source perspective concerns a speaker’s institutional position shaping what a source can establish. Compare that with the description asked for._
- ✅ independence

Full working after a second miss:

> Ghana’s independence preceded Nigeria’s.  
> So the answer is “independence”.  

Sources: decolonisation (Original labelled practice texts; UK Hansard Ghana and Nigeria independence debates; French diplomatic archives on West African constitutional change)

### `y5-1-assess` · Lesson 1 · spot the error, level 3

Prompt: Find the claim that misrepresents the history or the evidence.

Shows 3 correct and 1 wrong statement(s), drawn from:

- ✅ nationalist mobilisation: African organisation and pressure for self-government.
- ✅ constitutional advance: an expansion of internal governing responsibility before sovereignty.
- ✅ independence: the transfer to sovereign status in the Ghana case.
- ✅ source perspective: a speaker’s institutional position shaping what a source can establish.
- ❌ Ghanaian independence occurred without African political organisation.  
  _↳ African parties and mobilisation were central to the process._
- ❌ Internal self-government meant every colonial power had already disappeared.  
  _↳ Reserved authority distinguished autonomy from full sovereignty._
- ❌ Ghana became independent only after Nigeria in {1960}.  
  _↳ Ghana’s independence preceded Nigeria’s._
- ❌ A speech by one official proves unanimous popular consent.  
  _↳ Other voices and evidence are needed to assess wider opinion._


Three generated variants:

> Find the claim that misrepresents the history or the evidence.

- ◻️ nationalist mobilisation: African organisation and pressure for self-government.  
  _↳ explanation: This is an accurate distinction: African parties and mobilisation were central to the process. Look for a claim that reverses the event, relationship or limit._
- ◻️ independence: the transfer to sovereign status in the Ghana case.  
  _↳ explanation: This is an accurate distinction: Ghana’s independence preceded Nigeria’s. Look for a claim that reverses the event, relationship or limit._
- ✅ Ghana became independent only after Nigeria in 1960.  
  _↳ explanation: Ghana’s independence preceded Nigeria’s._
- ◻️ constitutional advance: an expansion of internal governing responsibility before sovereignty.  
  _↳ explanation: This is an accurate distinction: Reserved authority distinguished autonomy from full sovereignty. Look for a claim that reverses the event, relationship or limit._

Full working after a second miss:

### `y5-9-assess` · Lesson 9 · spot the error, level 3

Prompt: Find the claim that misrepresents the history or the evidence.

Shows 3 correct and 1 wrong statement(s), drawn from:

- ✅ industrial technology: wider application of steel, electricity and chemical processes.

- ✅ transport development: expanding networks and new transport forms with uneven access.
- ✅ military-industrial competition: industrial capacity contributing to arms and naval rivalry.
- ❌ Electricity and railways had no history before {1870}.  
  _↳ Their roots predated the period of wider adoption._
- ❌ Modern antibiotics were universally available across Europe before {1914}.  
  _↳ That is an anachronistic claim about a later treatment era._
- ❌ Faster transport eliminated every cost and inequality.  
  _↳ Infrastructure and access remained uneven._
- ❌ Industrial military expansion automatically guaranteed peace.  
  _↳ Competition could intensify insecurity rather than remove it._

### `y5-10-assess` · Lesson 10 · spot the error, level 3

Prompt: Find the claim that misrepresents the history or the evidence.

Shows 3 correct and 1 wrong statement(s), drawn from:

- ✅ production growth: an expansion of output through industrial organisation and technology.
- ✅ labour response: workers’ organisation and reform demands concerning industrial conditions.
- ✅ unequal global connections: industrial demand and transport linking regions through uneven economic relationships.
- ✅ environmental cost: damage associated with industrial production and urban concentration.
- ❌ Higher total output proves every person gained equally.  
  _↳ Distribution and working conditions require separate evidence._
- ❌ Workers had no agency in responding to industrial change.  
  _↳ Organisation and reform were important responses._
- ❌ Every colonial railway was designed equally around all local needs.  
  _↳ The purpose and distribution of benefits must be examined._
- ❌ Industrial growth had no possible effects on surroundings or health.  
  _↳ Pollution and living conditions formed part of its costs._

### `y5-12-assess` · Lesson 12 · spot the error, level 3

Prompt: Find the claim that misrepresents the history or the evidence.

Shows 3 correct and 1 wrong statement(s), drawn from:

- ✅ economic manifestation: an observable feature of the downturn such as unemployment or falling output.
- ✅ export vulnerability: exposure of an export-dependent economy to falling overseas demand or prices.
- ✅ policy response: action intended to address hardship or financial instability.
- ✅ political consequence with limits: political changes whose outcome depended on the particular setting.
- ❌ A depression is defined by every household enjoying rising employment.  
  _↳ The stated manifestations show contraction and hardship._
- ❌ A country needed its own major share market to be affected.  
  _↳ Trade could transmit hardship through export earnings._
- ❌ A policy announcement proves an immediate universal recovery.  
  _↳ Results require evidence and differed across measures and time._
- ❌ Every affected country inevitably became the same kind of dictatorship.  
  _↳ Economic crisis interacted with institutions and political choices._
